  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO fails to provide useful relative signals when all responses in a rollout group are incorrect. Privileged self-distillation fills this gap with dense token supervision, but persistent imitation throughout training causes the teacher to become a biased, low-variance surrogate for the reward, opposing reward-improving updates once the policy is capable of success. The authors aim to dynamically decide when to use imitation versus relative reward learning based on the policy's current capability.

Method: I-SDPO makes a per-instance routing decision shared across the rollout group: if all responses are incorrect, use a privileged self-distillation objective (imitation); if any response is correct, use the standard GRPO objective. This design confines imitation to scenarios where group-relative rewards are uninformative. A local analysis characterizes when teacher and reward directions align and shows that a non-vanishing biased distillation weight creates an optimization bias floor. The routing rule automatically reduces the expected distillation rate as success probability rises, removing the need for a scheduled decay.

Result: On the SciKnowEval benchmark, I-SDPO achieves the best results across all four scientific domains, improving average mean@16 accuracy from 56.67% (GRPO) to 70.31%, with a maximum domain gain of 18.24 percentage points.

Conclusion: I-SDPO effectively combines the strengths of GRPO and self-distillation by adaptively switching between them based on instance-level success. This avoids both the uninformative reward issue of GRPO and the biased teacher problem of persistent distillation, leading to substantial performance improvements on scientific knowledge evaluation tasks.

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within a rollout group, but receives no useful relative signal when every sampled response is incorrect. Privileged self-distillation can fill this gap with dense token supervision, yet applying it throughout training creates a different failure mode: the teacher is a biased, low-variance surrogate for the reward objective, so persistent imitation can oppose reward-improving updates after the policy becomes capable of producing successful trajectories. We introduce I-SDPO (Instance-Level Adaptive Self-Distillation Policy Optimization), which treats teacher reliance as capability-dependent. I-SDPO makes one routing decision per input instance and shares it across that instance's rollout group: all-incorrect groups use a privileged self-distillation objective, whereas any-success groups remain intact for GRPO. This design uses imitation only where group-relative rewards are uninformative. A local analysis characterizes when teacher and reward directions align and shows that a non-vanishing biased distillation weight induces an optimization bias floor. The routing rule automatically reduces the expected distillation rate as success probability rises, withdrawing teacher influence without a hand-designed schedule. On SciKnowEval, I-SDPO obtains the best result in all four scientific domains and improves average mean@16 accuracy from 56.67% with GRPO to 70.31%, with a maximum domain gain of 18.24 points.

</details>


### [298] [The Objective Is the Bottleneck: Latent World Models Encode What Their Planners Cannot Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12959)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 潜在世界模型的规划在长视距下失败，不是因为预测器退化，而是因为规划目标（平方潜在距离）与真实距离不对齐，导致成本函数饱和甚至随距离增加而下降。仅替换目标函数（无需重训练或GPU）即可将长视距成功率从26%提升至98%，使规划不再依赖视距。最优成本并非最精确的度量；基于可达性的目标比基于邻近性的目标规划得更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为潜在世界模型的规划失败源于预测器精度下降，但本文通过复现LeWorldModel在TwoRoom环境中的表现，证明真正的约束是规划器的目标函数。预测器在75步后的想象状态误差仅为假设世界冻结的0.189，而规划器从未想象超过25步；平方潜在距离目标与真实距离的相关性仅r=0.426，且在约80单位后饱和，120单位后反而下降，导致远离目标可降低成本。

Method: 本文系统分析了LeWorldModel在TwoRoom环境中的表现，通过探针实验证明嵌入中保留了完整的位置信息（R²=0.9922）。识别出病理是交叉熵方法规划最小化平方潜在距离所致。仅将目标函数替换为从帧分离学习到的可达性度量（无需重训练或GPU），其余部分保持不变。

Result: 仅替换目标函数后，偏移100处的目标达成率从26.0%提升至98.0%，与偏移25处持平（98.0%），并在三分之一预算下达到92.0%。规划不再依赖视距。可达性头在预测空间距离上弱于位置探针（r=0.819 vs 0.9897），但规划更好，跨越环境分隔墙时成本高24%（平方潜在距离仅高4%），表明它学习了可达性而非邻近性。

Conclusion: 潜在世界模型规划的长视距失败根源在于目标函数而非预测器。最优成本函数并非最精确的度量；基于可达性的目标可以极大提升规划性能，且无需额外训练或计算资源。规划器的目标选择比预测器精度更关键。

Abstract: Latent world models are judged by how well they predict, so when planning fails at long horizons the natural reading is that the predictor degrades. On a reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom we show the binding constraint is the planner's objective instead.
  The predictor is not the limit: its imagined state seventy-five environment steps ahead is still only 0.189 as wrong as assuming the world froze, while the planner never imagines beyond twenty-five. The objective is. Cross-entropy-method planning minimises squared latent distance, which tracks true distance at r = 0.426, saturates by about eighty arena units and decreases beyond a hundred and twenty, so moving away from the goal can lower the cost. The information is present throughout: a ridge probe recovers position from the frozen embedding at R^2 0.9922.
  The pathology is the method's, not one reimplementation's. It is present in the authors' released weights, and across four checkpoints long-horizon success rank-orders exactly with metric quality and inversely with prediction accuracy.
  Replacing only the objective, with nothing retrained and no GPU, lifts goals reached at offset 100 from 26.0% to 98.0%, equals the 98.0% at offset 25, and reaches 92.0% under a third of the budget: planning stops depending on the horizon. The best cost is not the most accurate. A head learned from frame separation alone predicts spatial distance worse than a position probe (r = 0.819 against 0.9897) yet plans better, charging 24% more to cross the environment's dividing wall where squared latent distance charges 4% less. It has learned reachability, not proximity.

</details>


### [299] [Understanding Backdoor Vulnerabilities in Vertical Federated Learning: The Gap Between Research and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12962)
*Ziqi Zhao,Jialin Lu,Junjie Shan,Junyuan Zhang,Shuya Yang,Ka-Ho Chow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了垂直联邦学习（VFL）中的后门攻击脆弱性，揭示了现有研究与实际部署之间存在显著差距：现有方法依赖不现实的先验知识且评估方法存在缺陷。作者重新定义了符合实际约束的威胁模型，提出了实用的后门攻击流程，并构建了后门攻击专用基准BVBench，证明当前对VFL后门风险的理解是脆弱的，为推动真实场景下的防御研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有文献报告了近乎完美的后门攻击成功率和有效的防御方法，但这些结果在现实条件下往往不成立，显示出研究与实际之间存在着根本性差距。现有工作忽视了关键的实际约束，并依赖不现实的先验知识，且这些局限性因评估实践设计不当而被掩盖。因此，有必要进行系统性、面向实践的重新研究。

Method: 本文提出：(1) 基于现实约束重新定义威胁模型；(2) 设计实用的后门攻击工作流程；(3) 构建后门攻击专用基准BVBench，包含最新的基线方法，支持公平、实用、全面的评估。

Result: BVBench提供了强有力的证据，表明当前对VFL后门风险的理解是脆弱的，并为引导研究走向发现实际漏洞和开发更有意义的防御奠定了坚实基础。

Conclusion: 现有VFL后门攻击与防御评估因忽视实际约束和评估缺陷而产生虚假的安全感；通过引入现实威胁模型和专用基准，能够弥合研究与实用之间的鸿沟，推动领域向更可信的方向发展。

Abstract: Vertical Federated Learning (VFL) enables organizations holding complementary features of shared entities to collaborate and train models. In this setting, the initiator can withhold information about the learning task, while other contributors participate without exposing their local datasets, creating an asymmetric information structure aligned with growing privacy demands. However, this asymmetry is a double-edged sword. Among various threats, backdoor attacks are particularly concerning because VFL not only enables malicious contributors to poison the model during training, but also allows them to activate the backdoor at inference time to manipulate predictions. Although prior work has reported near-perfect attack success rates and proposed effective defenses, we find that most findings fail to hold under realistic conditions, exposing a fundamental gap between research and practice. In this paper, we present a systematic, practice-oriented study of backdoor vulnerabilities in VFL, revealing this gap in both methodological design and evaluation practices. We show that existing approaches overlook key practical constraints and therefore rely on unrealistic prior knowledge. Furthermore, these limitations have remained hidden due to poorly designed evaluation practices in the literature. To bridge this gap, we redefine threat models under realistic constraints, propose practical backdoor workflows, and introduce BVBench, a backdoor-centric benchmark that enables fair, practical, and comprehensive evaluation, preloaded with state-of-the-art baselines. BVBench provides strong evidence of the fragility of the current understanding of VFL backdoor risks and establishes a foundation for steering research toward uncovering practical vulnerabilities and developing more meaningful defenses.

</details>


### [300] [Comment on "Modeling rapid language learning by distilling Bayesian priors into artificial neural networks"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12974)
*Orr Well,Idan Tarshish,Nur Lan,Roni Katzir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批评了McCoy & Griffiths (2025)声称通过MAML可以将贝叶斯先验蒸馏到人工神经网络中的观点。作者指出，在标准先验解释下，M&G的程序并未真正植入先验，只是初始化网络权重；在更宽松的解释下，系统整体被视为贝叶斯学习者也面临挑战；并且M&G的模型过拟合且泛化能力差，与真正的贝叶斯学习者不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批评和澄清McCoy & Griffiths (2025)关于通过MAML将贝叶斯先验蒸馏到ANN中的说法，指出其方法论和解释上的问题。

Method: 通过理论分析，从标准先验解释和更宽松的解释两个角度审视M&G的程序，并通过对比M&G模型与真正贝叶斯学习者的泛化表现来评估MAML近似贝叶斯学习的效果。

Result: M&G的程序并未在标准意义上植入先验；即使采用更宽松的解释，系统整体作为贝叶斯学习者也面临挑战；M&G的模型过拟合且泛化能力差，与真正的贝叶斯学习者不同。

Conclusion: M&G声称通过MAML将贝叶斯先验蒸馏到ANN中的说法不成立，其方法并未真正实现贝叶斯学习，且泛化表现不佳。

Abstract: McCoy & Griffiths (2025, henceforth M&G) suggest that a Bayesian prior can be distilled into Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs) through Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML, Finn et al., 2017). They support this empirically by showing that meta-trained networks demonstrate formal language learning abilities comparable to Yang & Piantadosi (2023)'s Bayesian learner, significantly outperforming standard ANNs. We point out that under the standard interpretation of a prior, M&G's procedure does not actually instill one; it merely initializes network weights favorably, leaving the objective function unchanged. We then consider a more permissive interpretation, where the system as a whole can be seen as implementing a Bayesian learner even without an explicit prior in the objective. We show that this interpretation faces nontrivial challenges. Finally, we assess how well MAML approximates the empirical results of Bayesian learning, showing that unlike genuine Bayesian learners, M&G's model overfits and generalizes poorly to unseen data.

</details>


### [301] [Learning the Mathematical Property for Designing Low Mutual Coherence Binary Sensing Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12982)
*Rekha,Santosh Singh,S. K. Neogy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的学习型传感矩阵构建方法，该方法不依赖数据集或特定应用，仅利用数学性质（互相关性）定义损失函数，生成具有低互相关性的二元传感矩阵，从而实现压缩感知中信号的完美恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩感知中传感矩阵的构建是关键挑战，理想矩阵需满足限制等距性、零空间性质、火花性质等NP-hard条件。传统方法计算复杂且依赖特定应用或数据集。本文旨在设计一种无需数据集、无需特定应用、利用数学约束的通用传感矩阵构建方法，以降低计算成本并提高信号恢复的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用神经网络框架构建二元传感矩阵，矩阵元素通过共享底层规则生成；损失函数直接基于互相关性（mutual coherence）这一数学性质定义，无需大规模训练数据；网络结构简单，通过优化互相关性来近似满足理想矩阵的约束条件。

Result: 该方法首次将数学性质直接用作神经网络损失函数来构建传感矩阵；生成的矩阵具有低互相关性，计算成本大幅降低；框架具有通用性和鲁棒性，且减少了存储需求；在无需数据集和特定应用的前提下实现了信号的完美恢复。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于神经网络且以互相关性为损失函数的传感矩阵构建方法，在计算效率、通用性和鲁棒性方面具有显著优势，为压缩感知中传感矩阵的构建提供了一种新颖、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this research work, we are constructing the sensing matrix, which is essential for the success of the compressive sensing technique. We have chosen a learning-based technique for the construction of the sensing matrix. The novelty and uniqueness of the proposed technique is that it does not use any data set and also does not use a specific application. It uses the mathematical property/constraint for the construction of the sensing matrix for the perfect recovery of the signal. The perfect recovery of signals is an old and still very challenging problem in real-world applications. In late 2000, compressive sensing became a popular mathematical tool for the perfect recovery of sparse signals. The core of the compressive technique is the construction of the sensing matrix, which satisfies certain special properties such as restricted isometry property (RIP), null space property (NSP), and spark property (SP). All these properties are NP-hard problems and hence computationally challenging to solve. For all practical purposes, the construction of the sensing matrix needs to achieve low mutual coherence to achieve the perfect recovery of the signals. We have used a neural network for the construction of the sensing matrix, and this framework constructs a binary sensing matrix with low mutual coherence. The entries in the matrix are generated through a shared underlying rule. The proposed architecture is simple and does not use large-scale training data sets. Such uniqueness and novelty bring a drastic reduction in computational cost, and also, for the first time in literature, the use of a mathematical property for defining the loss function. In this proposed research work, the mutual coherence property has been used in the neural network framework. Such a neural network framework brings generality, robustness, and reduces storage requirements.

</details>


### [302] [Balanced Adaptive Prototype Selection for Scalable TabPFN Inference on Large-Scale Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12989)
*Mahboobe Jadid,Melika Rezaye Garkani,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BAPS框架，通过构建紧凑的上下文表示，将TabPFN等预训练表格基础模型扩展到百万级数据集，实现约1953倍上下文压缩，在CPU环境下仍保持强预测性能与可靠校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练表格基础模型虽预测能力强，但受限于推理上下文长度，难以直接应用于大规模数据集。现有方法缺乏有效的上下文压缩机制，亟需一种不修改或重训练模型即可构建紧凑、信息保持的上下文的方法。

Method: 提出平衡自适应原型选择（BAPS）框架，联合优化代表性结构、信息决策边界、局部密度、类别平衡和特征空间多样性，从大规模数据中选择紧凑原型子集作为推理上下文，无需修改或重训练预训练模型。

Result: 在百万行HIGGS和SUSY数据集上，仅用512个原型即可保持强预测性能和可靠校准，实现约1953倍上下文压缩；所有实验在Intel Core i7 CPU + 16GB RAM上完成，无需GPU加速。

Conclusion: 有效的上下文构建机制是将预训练表格基础模型扩展到百万级数据集的实用途径，BAPS框架为此提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pretrained tabular foundation models have demonstrated strong predictive capability; however, their application to large-scale datasets remains constrained by the limited inference context. This paper introduces Balanced Adaptive Prototype Selection (BAPS), a framework for constructing compact, information-preserving contexts for scalable TabPFN inference. Without modifying or retraining the pretrained model, BAPS jointly preserves representative structure, informative decision boundaries, local density, class balance, and feature-space diversity. Experiments on the million-row HIGGS and SUSY datasets show that 512 prototypes retain strong predictive performance and reliable calibration, corresponding to an approximately 1,953-fold context compression. All experiments were conducted on an Intel Core i7 CPU with 16 GB RAM and no GPU acceleration. These findings establish effective context construction as a practical mechanism for extending pretrained tabular foundation models to million-scale datasets.

</details>


### [303] [Incremental Evaluation and Training in Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13023)
*Jakub Peleška,Gustav Šír*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前关系深度学习（RDL）评估方法使用静态单次数据集快照，忽略了真实数据库随时间演化的特性；为此提出增量式多轮评估与训练范式，通过概念漂移分析和增量微调策略，证明基于迁移学习的增量训练在时序鲁棒性和适应性上优于从头训练的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RDL基准测试仅依赖静态、单次数据集快照，无法反映模型在数据持续积累过程中的性能变化，忽视了真实世界数据库的时序演化特性，导致对模型时序鲁棒性和适应性的评估不足。

Method: 提出增量式多轮评估与训练范式：在多个大规模数据集上分析数据演化和模型训练动态，检测时序概念漂移；设计多种增量微调策略（如仅更新部分层、全模型微调等），并引入一种优先关注近期预测准确率的时序评估指标。

Result: 实验表明大多数预测任务存在时序概念漂移；增量微调模型（基于迁移学习）在所有场景下均一致优于从头训练的昂贵基线，验证了迁移学习在RDL中的可行性与高效性。

Conclusion: RDL模型应接纳增量式评估与训练范式以应对时序演化，采用迁移学习进行连续微调既能显著提升性能又能降低计算成本，为未来RDL在动态数据库上的应用提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) models multi-tabular databases as temporal heterogeneous graphs to enable end-to-end representation learning. However, prevailing RDL evaluation practices rely on static, single-episode dataset snapshots, overlooking the continuous, time-evolving nature of real-world databases. Consequently, current RDL benchmarks fail to capture how model performance changes as new data accumulates over time. To address this limitation, we introduce an incremental, multi-episode evaluation and training paradigm to assess and improve the temporal robustness and adaptability of state-of-the-art RDL models. Using established large-scale datasets, we examine data evolution and model training dynamics, demonstrating that temporal concept drifts occur in the majority of predictive tasks. We present multiple incremental training regimes for fine-tuning the models and demonstrate that transfer learning is both feasible and highly effective in the RDL setting. Alongside a new temporal evaluation metric that prioritizes near-future accuracy, we show that our incrementally fine-tuned models consistently outperform the standard, expensive, from-scratch trained baselines.

</details>


### [304] [On the global feature importance for interpretable and trustworthy heat demand forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13039)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种 ante-hoc 可解释人工智能方法，用于评估区域供热系统智能控制中热需求预测模型（如梯度提升）的全局特征重要性，结合 Partial Dependence、Accumulated Local Effects 和 SHAP 等不依赖特征排列或扰动的后验方法，以提高模型的可解释性和可信度，并讨论了结果在供热过程中的互补性与具体解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高机器学习模型在区域供热系统智能控制中的可解释性和可信度，应对遵守公共标准、客户满意度和责任风险等挑战。

Method: 采用四种方法：梯度提升的内在可解释性，以及三种后验方法——部分依赖图（Partial Dependence）、累积局部效应（Accumulated Local Effects）和 SHAP。所有方法均不假设特征排列或扰动，以避免引入随机不真实数据实例带来的偏差。

Result: 讨论了结果的互补性，并在区域供热过程背景下提供了具体解释。

Conclusion: 该 ante-hoc 可解释性方法能有效评估热需求预测模型的全局特征重要性，提升模型透明度，有助于区域供热系统的智能控制与管理。

Abstract: The paper introduces the ante-hoc Explainable AI methodology to assess the global feature importance of the Machine Learning models used for heat demand forecasting in intelligent control of District Heating Systems, with motivation to facilitate their interpretability and trustworthiness, hence addressing the challenges related to adherence to communal standards, customer satisfaction and liability risks. Methodology includes use of four different approaches, namely intrinsic interpretability of Gradient Boosting method and selected post-hoc methods, namely Partial Dependence, Accumulated Local Effects and SHAP. None of the selected methods assume feature permutation or perturbations which can introduce bias due to introduction of random unrealistic values of data instances. Discussion of results is provided, including the assessment of complementarities where applicable, with specific interpretations in context of the district heating processes.

</details>


### [305] [Latent On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13040)
*Guibin Zhang,Jiayang Lyu,Ran Sun,Xinlei Yu,Haoyu Zhao,Qibing Ren,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式在线自蒸馏（LOPD），通过让教师网络的特权上下文本身成为可端到端学习的隐式表征，克服传统OPSD方法对人工设计特权知识的依赖，在智能体工具使用和代码生成任务上取得超越OPSD、SDPO、Skill-SD、GRPO等方法的效果，并显著提升了学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏（OPSD）方法依赖设计者人为指定的特权知识（如答案、反馈、技能或轨迹），限制了端到端可学习性与可扩展性，难以支撑智能体持续自我进化的需求。因此需要一种让特权上下文本身成为可学习表征的方法。

Method: LOPD 将教师的特权上下文构建为可端到端学习的连续隐式令牌：从经验中检索相关经历，组合成连续隐式令牌以条件化教师网络；学生网络从任务与交互历史中生成轨迹，并在每个访问前缀处接收密集的令牌级监督信号；同时引入特权间隔目标函数（privileged-margin objective）来稳定和调节隐式上下文的学习。

Result: 实验表明LOPD在智能体工具使用和代码生成任务上优于RLVR、OPSD、SDPO、Skill-SD等代表性方法；且在不到30%的采样预算下超越了GRPO和Skill-SD。消融实验直接证明让特权上下文可学习是实现这些增益的必要条件。

Conclusion: LOPD 通过让特权上下文可端到端学习，朝着更可扩展、更自驱动的智能体进化范式迈出了一步。

Abstract: Enabling agents to learn from experience and internalize it into their policy has become a central problem in self-evolving AI. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers an effective pathway by using a privileged self-teacher to provide dense supervision on the student's own trajectories; however, existing methods still rely heavily on designer-specified privileged artifacts (e.g., answers, feedback, skills, or trajectories), limiting the end-to-end learnability and scalability required for continual self-improvement. In this work, we introduce Latent On-Policy Self-Distillation (LOPD), which, rather than proposing another hand-crafted OPSD variant with a newly prescribed form of privileged context, makes the teacher's privileged context itself learnable end-to-end from experience. Technically, LOPD retrieves relevant experiences and composes them into continuous latent tokens that condition a self-teacher, while the student generates trajectories from the task and interaction history and receives dense token-level supervision at every visited prefix. We further introduce a privileged-margin objective to stabilize and regulate the learning of latent context. Empirically, LOPD demonstrates (I) strong performance, outperforming RLVR and representative OPSD methods including OPSD, SDPO, and Skill-SD across both agentic tool use and code generation; and (II) high learning efficiency, surpassing GRPO and Skill-SD with less than 30% of their rollout budget. Ablation studies further provide direct evidence that making privileged context learnable is necessary for realizing these gains. Together, these results position LOPD as a step toward a more scalable and self-directed paradigm for agent evolution.

</details>


### [306] [A Multispectral Framework for the Detection of Calcium Carbide-Induced Ripening and Shelf-Life Estimation in Climacteric Fruits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13073)
*Gurbhit Chaurakoti,Harshit Kumar,Hani Kumar,Anurag Singh,Ram Asrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多光谱传感（AS7265x，410-940nm）结合XGBoost的非侵入式框架，用于区分自然/乙烯利催熟与工业级碳化钙（CaC2）催熟的芒果和香蕉，同时估算成熟进度（%）和剩余货架期（天）。在芒果上分类准确率达95%，香蕉上达81%，CaC2类召回率分别为0.67和0.74。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非法使用工业级碳化钙（CaC2）催熟芒果、香蕉等跃变型水果会残留砷、磷等有害物质，对人体健康构成显著风险。现有检测方法多为侵入式或实验室分析，亟需一种非侵入式、快速、准确的现场鉴别手段，以区分安全催熟（自然成熟或乙烯利催熟）与CaC2催熟的水果，同时评估其成熟度和货架期。

Method: 使用AS7265x三合一光谱传感器获取芒果和香蕉在18个离散波长（410–940nm可见-近红外范围）的光谱轮廓；通过特征工程整合方法间光谱方差、特定波长强度比以及温湿度等环境参数；采用主成分分析（PCA）降维，保留前5-7个主成分（>90%方差）；基于所得特征集训练三个独立的XGBoost学习算法，分别用于催熟方法分类、剩余货架期定量估计和成熟进度估算。

Result: 芒果样本的分类准确率为95%，CaC2类召回率为0.67；香蕉样本的分类准确率为81%，CaC2类召回率为0.74。PCA降维后前5-7个主成分保留了超过90%的光谱方差。CaC2处理样本在可见光区显示出更急剧的光谱强度下降，对应加速的叶绿素降解和类胡萝卜素发育。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于仪器和数据的非侵入式多光谱框架能有效区分CaC2催熟与安全催熟的水果，并定量评估其成熟进度和货架期，为食品安全现场检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Significant health risks are associated with the illegal, yet commonly practiced use of industrial-grade Calcium Carbide (CaC2) for ripening climacteric fruits like mango and banana, which leaves behind trace residues of arsenic and phosphorus. To address this, the proposed study explores a novel, non-invasive multispectral framework for distinguishing safely ripened fruits (naturally ripened and ethephon-induced) from calcium carbide-ripened samples, while also estimating their ripening progression (in percentage) and remaining shelf life (in days). The spectral profiles of mango (Mangifera indica) and banana (Musa acuminata) at 18 discrete wavelengths in the visible-near infrared (NIR) range (410 nm - 940 nm) are studied using the AS7265x spectral triad sensor. CaC2-treated samples exhibit sharper spectral intensity drops in the visible region, consistent with accelerated chlorophyll degradation and carotenoid development. To characterize these physiological changes, the feature engineering strategy integrates inter-method spectral variance, intensity ratios at distinct wavelengths, and environmental parameters including temperature and humidity. Dimensionality reduction using Principal Component Analysis (PCA) retains >90% of spectral variance within the first 5-7 components. The resulting feature set is used to train three independent eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost)-based learning algorithms for ripening method classification, along with quantitative estimation of remaining shelf life and ripening progression. A classification accuracy of 95% along with carbide class recall of 0.67 is observed for mango samples, while the model achieves an accuracy of 81% and carbide class recall of 0.74 for banana. This instrumentation and data-driven approach demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed non-invasive framework.

</details>


### [307] [Learning Discrete Decisions for MIPs with Constraint-Aware Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13079)
*Vincenzo Di Vito,Mehdi Taghizadeh,Deepjyoti Deka,Kaarthik Sundar,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为约束图扩散（CGD）的基于学习的框架，利用图生成扩散模型结合无训练的可行性投影算子，高效近似求解混合整数优化问题，在最优输电切换和离散投资组合优化等任务上相比学习基线显著提升可行性和解质量，相比最先进的MINLP数值求解器实现高达425倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数优化问题需要同时确定离散和连续决策，并满足复杂组合约束，计算挑战性极大。现有数值求解器处理大规模MINLP时效率低下，而纯学习方法难以保证解的可行性。因此需要一种既能学习离散结构又能保证可行性的高效近似求解方法。

Method: 提出CGD框架，核心包括：(1) 基于图的生成扩散模型，学习混合整数优化问题中的离散决策分布；(2) 在逆向扩散过程中集成无训练的可行性投影算子，无需额外训练即可将中间样本逐步引导至可行域；(3) 生成离散决策后，剩余连续优化问题可通过现有数值方法高效求解。该方法与具体问题无关，通过合适的投影算子可适应多种混合整数优化问题。

Result: 在最优输电切换（ACOPF）和离散投资组合优化两个任务上评估CGD。相比学习基线，CGD在可行性和解质量上均有显著提升；相比最先进的MINLP数值求解器，CGD实现了高达425倍的加速。

Conclusion: CGD作为一种问题无关的框架，能够有效处理混合整数优化问题中的离散组件，通过扩散模型学习与可行性投影相结合，在保证解质量的同时大幅提升求解效率，具有广泛的适用性和显著的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel learning-based approach to approximately solve instances of mixed-integer optimization problems. These problems are computationally challenging, as they require jointly determining discrete and continuous decisions while satisfying complex combinatorial constraints. The proposed method relies on a graph-based generative diffusion model that learns the discrete component of mixed-integer optimization problems while integrating a training-free feasibility projection operator directly into the reverse diffusion process to steer intermediate samples toward the feasible set throughout generation. Once the discrete decisions are generated, the remaining optimization reduces to a continuous problem that can be solved efficiently (relative to the original problem) using existing numerical methods. The resulting framework named Constrained Graph Diffusion (CGD), is problem-agnostic and can accommodate a broad class of mixed-integer optimization problems through suitable projection operators. We evaluate CGD on optimal transmission switching for ACOPF and discrete portfolio optimization, demonstrating substantial improvements in feasibility and solution quality over learning-based baselines while achieving speedups of up to $425\times$ over state-of-the-art numerical solvers for MINLPs.

</details>


### [308] [Sampling Luck Masquerades as Allocation Gain: Auditing Test-Time Budget Allocation for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13087)
*Jinhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper investigates whether non-uniform allocation of a fixed sampling budget across instances can improve the performance of neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) solvers that report the best of many sampled solutions. The authors find that in-distribution (uniform TSP-100), apparent gains of 2.2–2.6% from an oracle allocation are artifacts of in-sample evaluation; out-of-sample, these gains vanish (0.457, 0.015, -0.512 percent). However, under distribution shift (mixed uniform and clustered instances), guided allocation yields real improvements: 11.5% for AM and 12.0% for SymNCO, while POMO (more robust) shows no gain. They provide a correction procedure, reporting checklist, and release data/code.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neural combinatorial optimization solvers typically allocate the same number of samples per instance. The authors ask whether a non-uniform allocation of a fixed total budget could improve performance, and they audit the measurement methodology to avoid biased conclusions.

Method: The authors measure the effect of non-uniform sample allocation using three pretrained solvers (POMO, AM, SymNCO) on TSP-100. They compare in-sample (oracle allocation computed on stored samples) vs. out-of-sample evaluation. They also conduct a pre-registered confirmatory experiment under distribution shift (mixed uniform and clustered instances) using held-out sample statistics for allocation guidance. They develop a correction procedure and reporting checklist to avoid in-sample bias.

Result: In-distribution: oracle allocation suggests 2.2–2.6% gain in-sample, but out-of-sample the gains are indistinguishable from zero. Under distribution shift: allocation guided by held-out statistics improves best-of-k by 11.5% (AM) and 12.0% (SymNCO); POMO shows -0.3%. The gain exceeds a frozen distribution-label baseline by 4.2 points. Exploratory policy with 20-sample probe retains 3.4% (AM) and 4.6% (SymNCO).

Conclusion: Non-uniform sample allocation can provide real gains under distribution shift but not in-distribution. Naive in-sample evaluation overestimates gains; proper out-of-sample evaluation or correction procedures are necessary. The authors release a correction procedure, reporting checklist, and all data/code to enable reproducible research.

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) solvers report the best of many sampled solutions per instance, and the sample count is, by convention, identical for every instance. Whether a non-uniform allocation of a fixed total budget would buy anything has not been measured. We measure it, and we audit the measurement itself.
  First, on in-distribution workloads the allocation headroom is not detectable. Across three pretrained solvers (POMO, AM, SymNCO) on uniform TSP-100, an oracle allocation computed and evaluated on the same stored samples reports a 2.2-2.6% gain with intervals excluding zero; measured out of sample the same gain is indistinguishable from zero (0.457, 0.015, -0.512 percent). Following the customary in-sample procedure, all three solvers would have supported a published 2%-level gain that does not exist. We calibrate this bias against an instance-wise null in which the true gain is zero by construction; over the ranges we test it does not shrink with more samples or more instances.
  Second, the same correction that removes the phantom gains preserves a real one. Under distribution shift (a workload mixing uniform and clustered instances), a pre-registered confirmatory experiment finds that allocation guided by held-out sample statistics improves best-of-k by 11.5% (AM, primary endpoint; 95% CI [7.4, 19.7]) and 12.0% (SymNCO, replication) at equal evaluation budget, with the signal-acquisition cost not charged; a pre-registered negative control (POMO, an order of magnitude more robust to shift) shows -0.3% [-0.7, 0.24]. The gain exceeds a frozen distribution-label baseline by 4.2 points [1.9, 7.7]. An exploratory policy charging a 20-sample probe against the same budget retains 3.4% (AM) and 4.6% (SymNCO).
  We give a correction procedure and a reporting checklist, and release all data, code, and the pre-registration record.

</details>


### [309] [FlowLOB: Efficient and Controllable Limit Order Book Generation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13096)
*Zhuohan Wang,Andreea Bacalum,Ollie Olby,Carmine Ventre,Namid Stillman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowLOB 是一个基于条件流匹配的限价订单簿轨迹生成器，在多个 HKEX 股票和三种采样频率上训练，使用 tick-relative 表示，能够零样本迁移到未见过的标的。相比扩散模型，流匹配仅需 10 步 ODE 求解即可达到最优质量，而扩散需要更多步数。在细粒度采样频率下，FlowLOB 在多数分布指标上优于两种学习型和两种基于智能体的基线模型。通过反事实可控性测试，FlowLOB 在多数设定下满足条件变化驱动生成统计量向真实尾部区域移动的准则。其真实感和控制效果可零样本迁移到未见的股票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的限价订单簿模拟器（基于智能体模型和深度生成模型）只能部分满足实际应用所需的四个关键特性：真实的市场动态、高效的计算采样、可控的场景生成以及跨标的泛化能力。FlowLOB 旨在同时实现这些目标。

Method: FlowLOB 采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）生成 LOB 轨迹，以 tick-relative 表示在多个 HKEX 股票和三种采样频率（0.1s, 1s, 10s）上训练。由于流模型和扩散模型共享相同的公式化框架，作者在相同数据、架构和预算下训练两者，并通过相同的固定步数 ODE 求解器采样，实现公平比较。

Result: 流匹配仅需 10 步 ODE 求解即可达到最佳质量，而扩散模型需要更多函数评估才能达到相同保真度。在 0.1s 和 1s 采样频率下，FlowLOB 在大多数分布指标上优于两个学习型模型和两个基于智能体的基线。反事实可控性测试表明，FlowLOB 在多数设定下满足条件变化驱动生成统计量向真实尾部区域移动的准则。真实感和控制效果可零样本迁移到未见的股票。此外还进行了网络架构和学习率的消融研究。

Conclusion: FlowLOB 通过流匹配方法在采样效率、生成真实感、可控泛化能力上取得了更好的平衡，尤其在高频场景下显著优于现有方法，并且具备零样本迁移能力，为 LOB 模拟提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Limit order book (LOB) simulators are most useful to practitioners when they combine realistic market dynamics, computationally efficient sampling, controllable scenario generation, and the ability to generalize beyond the instruments seen during training---properties that existing agent-based and deep generative simulators provide only partially. We present \textbf{FlowLOB}, a conditional \textbf{flow}-matching generator of \textbf{LOB} trajectories, trained on multiple Hong Kong Exchange (HKEX) symbols at three sampling frequencies ($0.1$s, $1$s, $10$s) in tick-relative representation that transfers to unseen instruments. Because flow and diffusion models admit a common formulation, we train both with identical data, architecture, and budget, and sample both through the same fixed-step ODE solvers, yielding a controlled comparison of sampling efficiency and fidelity. Flow matching attains its best quality with only $10$ ODE-solver steps, whereas diffusion needs many more function evaluations to approach the same fidelity. At this efficient operating point, FlowLOB improves realism over baselines, two learned and two agent-based models, in most distributional metrics at the two finer sampling frequencies. We evaluate counterfactual controllability with a distributional test that asks whether changing a scenario condition moves the generated statistic toward the corresponding real tail regime; FlowLOB satisfies this criterion in most tested settings. Both realism and control effects transfer zero-shot on a held-out symbol. We additionally conduct ablation studies on the network architecture and the learning rate.

</details>


### [310] [Branch and Bound for Relational Verification of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13118)
*Kota Fukuda,Zhenya Zhang,Guanqin Zhang,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分支定界（BaB）的神经网络验证框架，专门处理关系规约（如全局鲁棒性）。该框架通过拆分关系神经元而非单个神经元，并利用对偶形式选择最优关系神经元进行拆分，有效解决了现有抽象方法不完整导致的误报问题。在817个验证问题上的实验表明，SaBRe在求解数量和验证效率上均优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI组件在信息物理系统（CPS）中的广泛应用，验证神经网络满足关系规约（例如全局鲁棒性）对于安全关键应用至关重要。现有的基于凸过近似的抽象技术虽然可靠但具有固有的不完整性，可能产生误报，因此需要有效的抽象细化方法。

Method: 提出了一种分支定界（BaB）框架，通过迭代拆分问题直至所有子问题被验证。核心创新包括：1）拆分关系神经元而非传统方法中的单个神经元；2）基于验证问题的对偶形式设计关系神经元选择策略，高效选出最可能带来最大细化的最优关系神经元。

Result: 在ACAS Xu、MNIST-F、MNIST-C、CIFAR和GTSRB五个数据集上的817个验证问题中，所提方法SaBRe在求解的问题数量和验证效率方面均优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的关系神经元拆分与对偶引导的选择策略有效缓解了抽象细化的难题，显著提升了关系规约验证的性能。

Abstract: Verification of neural networks against relational specifications, such as global robustness, is crucial for safety-critical applications of cyber-physical systems (CPS), given their increasing adoption of AI components. Compared to simple trace properties (e.g., local robustness), verifying relational specifications requires reasoning about the relationship between multiple network inferences, which brings significant technical challenges. Existing research has explored abstraction techniques based on sound and convex over-approximation of neural network outputs; however, since these approaches are inherently incomplete and may raise false alarms, they further underscore the need of effective abstraction refinement.
  In this paper, we propose a branch-and-bound (BaB) framework to mitigate the issue, which iteratively splits the problem until all sub-problems are verified. Specifically, our BaB framework features splitting of relational neurons rather than individual neurons as prior works do, and as the core of our technique, we devise a relational neuron selection strategy based on the dual formulation of the verification problem, which allows us to efficiently select the (most likely) optimal relational neuron that maximizes the refinement brought by problem splitting. We evaluate SaBRe on 817 verification problems across ACAS Xu, MNIST-F, MNIST-C, CIFAR and GTSRB. The results show that SaBRe outperforms different baseline approaches, in terms of the number of solved instances and verification efficiency, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our proposed techniques.

</details>


### [311] [ProME: Prototype-Margin Environments with Repair-Aware Selection for Group-Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13190)
*Qianqian Wang,Yunshan Li,Dawei Huang,Wenwu Gong,Lili Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProME是一种无需训练组标签的组鲁棒学习方法，通过将原型边际在中位数处拆分来构建近似平衡的环境，并拟合组平衡线性头来评估候选模型，实现了在推断环境与部署预测器之间的对齐，在理论上给出了最差风险的界限，并在实验中取得了最高的平均最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常从独立的参考模型中推断环境，并在拟合部署使用的分类器之前选择表示，这两个决策与部署的预测器不一致，导致次优的组鲁棒性。

Method: 将无训练组标签的组鲁棒性形式化为内生环境与修复感知选择（ERAS）问题，提出ProME方法：沿训练轨迹将原型边际在中位数处拆分以构建近似平衡的环境，在带组标注的验证数据上拟合组平衡线性头来对候选预测器进行排序，并通过最差组准确率进行评估。

Result: 理论证明了固定预测器和划分下推断环境的最差风险界限，并在显式对齐条件下该界限可迁移到真实组。实验表明原型边际丰富了捷径冲突样本，分类器修复重塑了候选评估，ProME在相同组标签访问条件下取得了最高的平均最差组准确率。

Conclusion: ProME通过对齐环境推断与预测器决策，有效提升了无需训练组标签的组鲁棒学习性能，在理论和实验上均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Group-robust learning is crucial for maintaining accuracy on rare subpopulations when training-group labels are unavailable. However, existing methods often infer environments from a separate reference model and select representations before fitting the classifier used at deployment, leaving both decisions misaligned with the deployed predictor. In this work, we formulate group robustness without training-group labels as the endogenous environments with repair-aware selection (ERAS) problem, and propose ProME (Prototype-Margin Environments) to align both decisions with the deployed predictor. ProME splits prototype margins at their median to construct approximately balanced environments along the training trajectory, and fits a group-balanced linear head on group-annotated validation data to rank the resulting predictors by validation worst-group accuracy. We theoretically bound the worst risk across the inferred environments for a fixed predictor and partition, showing that this bound transfers to the oracle groups under an explicit alignment condition. Extensive experiments show that prototype margins enrich shortcut-conflicting examples, classifier repair reshapes candidate evaluation, and ProME achieves the highest average worst-group accuracy among the compared methods with the same group-label access.

</details>


### [312] [Beyond Simulated Benchmarks: Evaluating Motion Representations for Fall Detection Under Real-World Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13197)
*Timilehin B. Aderinola,Ilaria D'Ascanio,Luca Palmerini,Lorenzo Chiari,Jochen Klenk,Clemens Becker,Brian Caulfield,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种运动表示方法（区间、核、符号、基础模型）在真实世界数据稀缺下的可穿戴跌倒检测性能，发现高度参数化的核和基础模型在模拟数据上表现优异但在真实稀缺和域迁移下严重退化；区间表示在真实数据上绝对性能最强，而符号表示结合物理冲击描述在域迁移下降级最小且在极端稀缺下保持检测灵敏度但精度较低。强调了超越模拟基准评估的重要性，表示选择对部署关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界跌倒数据极其稀缺（收集100次跌倒需约10万天监测），导致标注数据有限，现有方法多依赖模拟数据集，在实验室中表现良好但真实泛化能力差。因此需要系统性评估不同运动表示在真实数据稀缺条件下的鲁棒性，以指导可部署跌倒检测系统的表示选择。

Method: 利用加速度计信号，比较了区间表示（interval-based）、核表示（kernel-based）、符号表示（symbolic）和基础模型表示（foundation model）。额外提出了一种轻量级符号表示，将短运动片段转换为符号句子并附加物理接地冲击描述符。实验使用模拟跌倒数据集FallAllD和临床验证的真实跌倒数据集FARSEEING，通过交叉验证、控制数据稀缺性和跨数据集迁移来评估表示选择对鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 高度参数化的核模型和基础模型在模拟数据上表现优异，但在数据稀缺和域迁移下性能严重下降。区间表示在真实世界数据上取得最强的绝对性能。通过物理接地冲击描述符增强的符号表示在域迁移下性能下降最小，且在极端稀缺条件下保持检测灵敏度，但精度较低。

Conclusion: 表示选择对于在真实世界数据稀缺条件下部署跌倒检测系统至关重要，必须超越模拟基准进行评估。研究结果强调了评估真实世界泛化能力的重要性，并为未来在数据稀缺条件下的可穿戴跌倒检测表示选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Falls are a major health concern for older adults, and wearable sensors have been widely explored for detecting falls and enabling timely intervention. However, real-world falls are extremely rare: collecting 100 of them requires an estimated 100,000 days of monitoring, resulting in severely limited labelled data for training machine learning models. Consequently, many approaches rely on simulated datasets, often reporting high laboratory performance but limited real-world generalisation. We present a systematic evaluation of motion representations for wearable fall detection under real-world data scarcity. Using accelerometer signals, we compare interval-based, kernel-based, symbolic, and foundation model representations. As an interpretable baseline, we additionally investigate a lightweight symbolic representation that converts short motion segments into symbolic sentences augmented with physically-grounded impact descriptors. Experiments use FallAllD, a simulated falls dataset, and FARSEEING, a clinically verified real-world falls dataset. Through cross-validation, controlled data scarcity, and cross-dataset transfer, we examine how representation choices affect robustness under realistic deployment. Our results reveal that highly parameterised kernel and foundation models excel on simulated data but degrade severely under both data scarcity and domain shift. Although the interval-based representation achieves the strongest absolute real-world performance, augmenting a symbolic representation with physically-grounded impact descriptors yields the smallest degradation under domain shift and retains detection sensitivity under extreme scarcity, albeit at lower precision. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating beyond simulated benchmarks and show that representation choice is critical for deployable fall detection given the scarcity of real-world data.

</details>


### [313] [TANGCO: Learning Topology-Aware Capacity Allocation for Overload-driven Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13212)
*Orkun Irsoy,Leman Akoglu,Osman Yagan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对网络级联故障中容量分配问题（非连续、不可微的目标函数），提出TANGCO方法，利用图神经网络策略结合策略梯度和启发式锚点进行优化。在大量合成和真实网络上，TANGCO显著优于人工设计的启发式方法，具备良好的泛化性、可迁移性和扩展性，并揭示出何时可简化为局部风险启发式、何时需拓扑感知策略的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电力网、交通网、云计算集群等网络系统中，节点容量有限，过载会导致故障级联。如何在固定总容量预算下分配各节点容量以最大程度抵抗级联故障是关键问题。但该优化问题的目标函数非连续、不可导，精确优化和梯度方法均不适用，且尚无已知的最优分配策略。

Method: 提出TANGCO（拓扑感知神经图引导容量优化），使用图神经网络（GNN）作为策略网络，通过级联模拟器进行策略梯度学习，并引入一个启发式锚点辅助训练。该方法在模拟环境中学习容量分配策略，无需可微目标函数。

Result: 在5类合成图族和5个真实网络（电力、道路、航空、互联网）上评估：TANGCO在全部450个合成实例和45个真实网络条件中的40个上优于四种人工启发式方法的最优值，鲁棒性提升1.6%~246%。学习到的策略可在同族未知图上泛化，部分跨拓扑迁移；预训练模型TANGCO^pre在未见真实网络上达到逐网训练效果。训练规模近线性增长，预训练模型无需再训练即可部署，成本与启发式方法相当。无GNN的自由向量变体性能仅接近启发式，表明图表示是关键增益来源。

Conclusion: 对学到的分配策略进行分析，识别出局部风险足够使用的条件，由此提出改进的闭式启发式方法，并揭示了必须使用拓扑感知学习策略的场景。总体表明TANGCO是解决网络容量抗级联分配问题的有效且实用的方法。

Abstract: Networked systems, from power grids to traffic networks and cloud clusters, carry loads across nodes with limited capacity. A node whose load exceeds its capacity fails and sheds its load onto its neighbors, which can trigger a system-wide cascade. We study how to allocate a fixed capacity budget across nodes to resist these cascades under local load redistribution. The problem is difficult because no optimal allocation is known, and the fail-or-survive objective is non-differentiable and piecewise constant, so exact and gradient-based optimization methods do not directly apply. We introduce TANGCO (Topology-Aware Neural Graph-Guided Capacity Optimization), which uses a graph neural network policy trained through the cascade simulator with policy-gradient learning and a heuristic anchor. We evaluate TANGCO on five synthetic graph families and five real networks spanning power, road, air, and Internet topologies. The learned policy improves on the best of four hand-designed heuristics in all 450 synthetic instances and in 40 of 45 real-network conditions, with robustness gains ranging from 1.6% to 246%. The learned policies transfer to unseen graphs within a family and partially across related topologies, and TANGCO$^{pre}$, pre-trained on synthetic graphs, matches per-network training on unseen real networks. Training scales near-linearly with graph size, and TANGCO$^{pre}$ allocates on a new network with no per-target training, matching the deployment cost of a hand-designed heuristic. Free-vector variants without the GNN, stay close to the heuristics, so the graph representation carries the gain beyond numerical search. Finally, analysis of the learned allocations identifies when local risk is sufficient, leads to an improved closed-form heuristic, and reveals the regimes where a topology-aware learned policy remains necessary.

</details>


### [314] [History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13215)
*Tianshuo Zhang,Xianglei Xing,Wenzhe Zhai,Jia Gao,He Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks (HiLNN)，利用历史位置序列通过递归编码器提取潜在上下文，同时重构未观测的初始速度并自适应调整拉格朗日系统的质量矩阵、势能和阻尼系数，结合可微 RK4 滚动方案进行端到端多步轨迹监督与能量一致性正则化训练，在保守、耗散及异质变参数系统中相比现有方法实现了更优的长期预测精度和能量保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从仅位置观测中预测机械系统的长期演化极具挑战，因为隐藏的速度和轨迹特定的物理参数必须同时推断。现有的拉格朗日神经网络 (LNN) 虽能保证物理合理性，但通常需要完整状态输入且缺乏对变化系统参数的适应性。HiLNN 旨在突破这些限制。

Method: HiLNN 基于时间位置序列隐含编码动力学信息的思想，采用递归编码器从历史序列中提取潜在上下文。该上下文一方面用于重构未观测的初始速度，另一方面自适应地调制拉格朗日系统的质量矩阵、势能和阻尼系数。通过可微的 RK4 滚动方案，整个流水线在多步轨迹监督和能量一致性正则化下进行端到端优化。

Result: 在保守系统、耗散系统以及异质变参数系统上的实验表明，HiLNN 相比当前最先进的基线方法，在长期预测精度上表现更优，并能精确维持能量曲线。

Conclusion: HiLNN 通过历史信息隐式编码动力学并自适应调节系统参数，有效解决了仅位置观测下长期预测的难题，在多种物理系统中均取得领先性能。

Abstract: Forecasting the long-horizon evolution of mechanical systems from position-only observations is a pivotal yet difficult task, as hidden velocities and trajectory-specific physical properties must be inferred simultaneously. Although physics-guided neural networks like Lagrangian Neural Networks (LNNs) guarantee physical plausibility, they generally require complete state inputs and lack adaptability to changing system parameters. To break these limitations, we introduce History-informed Lagrangian Neural Networks (HiLNN). Grounded in the insight that temporal position sequences implicitly encode underlying dynamics, HiLNN employs a recurrent encoder to extract a latent context from history. This context not only reconstructs the unobserved initial velocity but also adaptively modulates the mass matrix, potential energy, and damping coefficients of a structured Lagrangian system. By leveraging a differentiable RK4 rollout scheme, the entire pipeline is optimized end-to-end under multi-step trajectory supervision and energy-consistency regularization. Empirical evaluations across conservative, dissipative, and heterogeneous variable-parameter systems show that HiLNN delivers superior long-term prediction accuracy and maintains precise energy profiles compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/yingtian22/History-informed-LNN.

</details>


### [315] [Knowledge-guided Pattern Discovery via Coupled Tensor Factorizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13234)
*Gaute Johannessen,Geert Roelof van der Ploeg,Evrim Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识引导的张量分解方法，通过联合分析真实数据和计算模型生成的模拟数据（线性耦合），在噪声代谢组学数据中提升模式发现性能，并揭示数据与模型之间的潜在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统（如人类代谢组或大脑）产生的多模态数据常呈现多路结构（如受试者×代谢物×时间），而张量分解虽能揭示可解释模式但主要基于数据驱动。现有计算模型蕴含丰富的先验知识，如何将模型知识融入多路数据分析以提升模式发现效果是核心动机。

Method: 提出知识引导的耦合张量分解方法，通过线性耦合机制将真实实验数据与计算模型生成的模拟数据联合分析，在分解过程中利用模拟数据中的先验信息引导真实数据的模式提取。

Result: 在真实代谢组学测量数据上的实验表明，用模拟数据引导噪声数据的分析能显著提升模式发现性能，同时还能揭示真实数据与计算模型之间的潜在不一致性。

Conclusion: 知识引导的耦合张量分解能有效整合数据驱动与模型驱动两种范式，在提高模式发现能力的同时提供数据-模型差异的检测手段，为复杂系统多路数据分析开辟新途径。

Abstract: In order to understand complex systems such as the human metabolome or human brain, different sensing technologies are used, generating complex data. These datasets are often multiway, i.e., with more than two axes of variation such as a subjects by metabolites by time array. While tensor factorizations have successfully revealed interpretable patterns from such complex data, they have so far been mainly data-driven. On the other hand, there is more to data -- there are computational models (of these systems), which are rich sources of prior information. In this paper, we introduce a knowledge-guided approach that brings together data and computational models by jointly analyzing real data and simulated data (generated using a computational model) using coupled tensor factorizations with linear coupling. Our experiments on real metabolomics measurements demonstrate that guiding the analysis of such noisy data with simulated data improves the pattern discovery performance while also revealing potential discrepancies between data and computational models.

</details>


### [316] [Novel Knowledge-Guided Generative Methods for Synthetic Transcriptomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13256)
*Francesca Pia Panaccione,Sofia Mongardi,Marco Masseroli,Pietro Pinoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种生成模型在转录组数据合成中的应用，重点探索通过基因图融入先验生物学知识的策略，并提出了三种GAN变体。其中，MK-TGAN（一种基于多核图神经网络的模型）在生成数据的真实性和实用性方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学研究越来越依赖数据密集型工具，但数据集的质性和可用性面临不平衡、偏差以及伦理/法律约束等挑战，限制了高质量数据的获取。合成数据生成有望克服这些限制。

Method: 对转录组数据的生成模型进行比较分析，研究通过基因图融入先验生物学知识的策略。具体引入并基准测试了三种生成对抗网络（GAN）变体，其中MK-TGAN是一种创新的多核图神经网络模型，利用先验知识图来生成数据。

Result: 先验知识整合策略能提升性能；MK-TGAN在生成数据的真实性和实用性方面均优于其他方法，持续产生具有更高真实度和生物学合理性的合成样本。

Conclusion: 融入先验生物学知识能够改善生成模型的性能，而MK-TGAN作为基于图神经网络的多核GAN变体，在转录组数据合成任务中表现最为出色。

Abstract: As biomedical research increasingly relies on data-intensive tools, the quality and utility of datasets are critical. Challenges such as imbalances, biases, and ethical or legal constraints often limit access to high-quality data. Synthetic data generation can help overcome these limitations. Here, we present a comparative analysis of generative models for transcriptomic data, investigating strategies to incorporate prior biological knowledge via gene graphs. This ensures that synthetic data capture real-world gene patterns, maintaining their usefulness for downstream tasks. In particular, we introduce and benchmark three variants of the Generative Adversarial Network. Among the alternatives, MK-TGAN - an innovative multi-kernel, Graph Neural Network-based model - stands out for its performance in terms of both the realism and utility of the generated data. Unlike other methods, MK-TGAN leverages prior knowledge graphs by exploiting graph neural networks. Our results show that prior knowledge integration strategies improve performance, and that MK-TGAN consistently produces synthetic samples with superior realism and biological plausibility.

</details>


### [317] [Virtual Temperature Sensors in Power Transformers Using Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13260)
*Berk Hadzhamolla,Alexander Johannes Stasik,Signe Riemer-Sørensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理感知的神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）框架，用于从实际时间序列数据中预测变压器热行为，该框架通过将简化热传递方程直接集成到Neural ODE中，实现了对异构变压器单元的标准化、鲁棒且物理一致的预测，并在挪威不同地区的15台变压器数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变压器热行为建模方法存在局限：数值方法（FEM/CFD）精度高但计算昂贵且难以实时应用；集总参数热模型依赖特定热常数且无法捕捉动态响应；纯数据驱动方法需要大量高质量数据且可能产生物理不一致或不可解释的结果。因此需要一种兼具物理一致性和计算效率的标准化预测框架。

Method: 开发了一种物理感知的神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）框架，将简化的热传递方程直接集成到Neural ODE公式中，利用连续时间建模能力实现平滑轨迹预测，并基于真实世界时间序列数据进行训练。

Result: 在来自挪威不同地区的15台具有不同设计和冷却机制的变压器数据集上进行了评估，结果表明该框架为异构变压器单元提供了一种标准化、物理感知且鲁棒的预测方法。

Conclusion: Neural ODE框架结合物理先验知识，能够有效预测变压器热行为，兼顾物理一致性与数据驱动灵活性，适用于大规模异构变压器场景。

Abstract: Accurate modeling and forecasting of power transformer thermal behavior are critical for reliability, asset lifetime, and optimized power system operation. Numerical approaches such as finite element methods (FEM) and computational fluid dynamics (CFD) offer high fidelity but are computationally expensive, require complex mesh generation, and are often impractical for real-time or large-scale applications, particularly when transformer geometries are unknown. Lumped-parameter thermal models are more practical but depend on transformer-specific thermal constants and may fail to capture dynamic responses under varying operating and environmental conditions. Purely data-driven machine learning methods, including artificial neural networks, convolutional neural networks, and long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, have shown success in forecasting transformer temperatures but typically require large volumes of high-quality training data and may produce physically inconsistent or uninterpretable results. This paper develops a physics-aware Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE) framework for forecasting transformer thermal behavior from real-world time-series data. Neural ODEs model system dynamics in continuous time, providing smooth trajectory prediction and a natural representation of continuously evolving thermal dynamics. A key contribution is the integration of simplified heat-transfer equations directly into the Neural ODE formulation. The model is evaluated across datasets from fifteen transformers in different regions of Norway with varying designs and cooling mechanisms. The results demonstrate that the developed Neural ODE framework provides a standardized, physics-aware, and robust forecasting approach for heterogeneous transformer units.

</details>


### [318] [Into the ORBIT for Time Series: Training Regimes for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13262)
*Hongjie Xia,Yiding Liu,Yifan Hu,Peiyuan Liu,Zewei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ORBIT训练范式，通过Bootstrap多级采样和全范围增量训练来控制预训练分布，训练了Falcon-2.0（一种简单的单变量编码器-only Transformer），并引入Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment训练目标，在零样本时间序列预测中取得了强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要聚焦于架构创新，而对大规模异构语料库的训练机制探索不足，导致预训练分布中领域不平衡、上下文需求、预测视野和缺失值模式难以控制。

Method: 提出ORBIT（全范围Bootstrap增量训练）范式，包含Bootstrap多级采样（控制数据集暴露，采样记录、目标变量、上下文窗口和预测视野）和全范围增量训练（在单个训练阶段变化上下文长度和预测视野）。基于此训练Falcon-2.0（单变量编码器-only Transformer），采用缺失感知的三通道分块标记化和并行分块预测，并引入Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment目标（利用深层表示作为浅层的停止梯度教师，不增加推理成本）。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准上，Falcon-2.0在跨领域和跨频率的零样本预测中展现了强劲性能。

Conclusion: ORBIT范式有效控制了预训练分布，使得简单架构也能在时间序列基础模型中取得优异表现，为未来TSFM的训练策略设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have advanced primarily through architectural innovation, while training regimes for large-scale heterogeneous corpora remain under-explored. As a result, pre-training distributions are often poorly controlled with respect to domain imbalance, context requirements, prediction horizons, and missingness. We introduce ORBIT (Omni-Range Bootstrap Incremental Training), a training paradigm that makes this distribution explicit and controllable. ORBIT combines Bootstrap Multi-Level Sampling, which controls dataset exposure and samples records, target variables, context windows, and prediction horizons, with Omni-Range Incremental Training, which varies context lengths and prediction horizons throughout a single training stage. Under ORBIT, we train Falcon-2.0, a simple univariate encoder-only Transformer with missingness-aware triple-channel patch tokenization and parallel patch prediction. We further introduce Rank-Guided Cross-Depth Alignment, a training objective that uses late-layer representations as stop-gradient teachers for shallow layers without additional inference cost. Evaluations on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench demonstrate strong zero-shot forecasting performance across diverse domains and frequencies.

</details>


### [319] [EEG Decoding Using CNN and LSTM Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13285)
*Athanasios Karagounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合卷积神经网络（CNN）和双向长短期记忆网络（bi-LSTM）的混合深度学习架构，用于直接从原始运动想象脑电图（MI-EEG）中学习高层次的时空表征，并建模时间依赖性。在公开和私有数据集上的实验表明，该架构在二类和三类运动想象分类任务中均表现出稳健的性能，并具有有前景的跨被试解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动想象脑机接口为中风或神经退行性疾病患者提供了灵活的通讯途径，但MI-EEG信号存在大量噪声且与大脑活动之间关系复杂、信息弱，导致可靠解码困难。尽管深度学习能从EEG中直接学习表征，其在MI-EEG特征学习中的应用仍相对有限。

Method: 提出一种混合深度学习架构，首先使用CNN直接从原始MI-EEG记录中学习高层次的空间和时间表征，然后利用bi-LSTM对提取的特征之间的时间依赖关系进行建模，从而结合CNN的局部特征提取能力和bi-LSTM的时序建模能力。

Result: 在公开数据集和自研EEG采集系统获得的私有数据集上进行了评估。实验结果表明，CNN&bi-LSTM架构在二类和三类运动想象分类中均具有稳健性能，并在所评估的方法中展现出有前景的跨被试（subject-independent）解码能力。

Conclusion: 该混合架构有效提升了MI-EEG解码的稳健性和跨被试泛化能力，为运动想象脑机接口的实际应用提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Motor imagery (MI) brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) have emerged as a promising approach for establishing flexible communication pathways between the human brain and external devices , particularly for individuals affected by stroke or neurodegenerative disorders. Reliable decoding of motor-imagery electroencephalography (MI-EEG) remains challenging because EEG recordings contain substantial noise and exhibit complex, weakly informative relationships with the underlying brain activity. Although deep learning provides an effective means of learning representations directly from EEG signals, its application to MI-EEG feature learning remains comparatively limited. This study introduces a hybrid deep-learning architecture that integrates a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a bidirectional long short-term memory (bi-LSTM) network. The CNN is used to learn high-level spatial and temporal representations directly from raw MI-EEG recordings, whereas the bi-LSTM models temporal dependencies and relationships among the extracted features. The proposed approach is evaluated using both a publicly available dataset and a privately acquired dataset obtained with an EEG acquisition system. The experimental results indicate that the CNN\&bi-LSTM architecture provides robust performance for both two- and three-class motor-imagery classification and demonstrates promising subject-independent decoding capability across the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [320] [Large-scale Testing Global Optimization Methods with Black-box Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13296)
*Wojciech Zarzecki,Jarosław Arabas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将黑盒对抗攻击（BBAA）问题作为高维全局优化基准测试的新思路，并验证了多种进化算法和元启发式方法在解决此类问题上的有效性，旨在推动全局优化方法与现代机器学习需求相融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局优化基准测试规模有限，且依赖少量源自上世纪70年代的分析函数，可能导致全局优化方法的发展产生偏向。需要更贴近现代机器学习实际挑战（如高维、黑盒、对抗性）的基准测试。

Method: 论证黑盒对抗攻击（BBAA）任务可作为高维全局优化基准测试；采用多种进化算法和其他元启发式方法求解示例BBAA问题，并评估其效率。

Result: 所测试的进化算法和元启发式方法在求解BBAA问题上展现出较高的效率，表明BBAA任务能够有效区分不同优化算法的性能。

Conclusion: BBAA任务可作为高维全局优化的有价值基准测试，有助于推动全局优化方法适应现代机器学习领域的需求，促进两个领域的交叉融合。

Abstract: Existing global optimization benchmark suites are of a moderate size and are based on a small number of analytical functions that date back even to the 1970s. This causes a risk of biasing the development of global optimization methods. We argue that the tasks related to the black-box adversarial attack (BBAA) can serve as valuable global optimization benchmark in many-dimensional space. We demonstrate the efficiency of several types of evolutionary algorithms and other metaheuristics in solving example BBAA problems. Thus, we take a step towards convergence of global optimization methods to the challenges and needs that arise in the modern machine learning field.

</details>


### [321] [The Time Value of Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13297)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Lineage-Value Policy Gradients (LVPG)的长期信用分配框架，用于进化搜索中的交易策略发现。该框架通过将搜索控制解耦为专用策略头（bootstrapped critic和actor head），在有限时域内利用世系价值（lineage value）来指导突变强度，从而比即时回报优化更快找到高适应度策略，在90组配对实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在进化搜索中，弱个体可能成为有价值的祖先，但即时回报控制会忽略这种延迟效用，惩罚那些当前表现不佳但能开启高产未来谱系的突变。现有方法缺乏对长期信用分配的考虑，导致搜索效率受限。

Method: 将进化搜索中的延迟效用形式化为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程中的时间价值，并提出LVPG框架：采用共享生成骨干网络，解耦为两个专用头——bootstrapped critic通过多步突变树估计有限时域世系潜力值，actor根据剩余搜索预算动态调整突变强度。通过90组配对实验（控制算子、谱系监督、折叠、种子、预算等变量）隔离长期信用分配的影响。

Result: 路径基础的信用分配显著加速有限预算搜索，验证集最佳AUC提升0.394 Sharpe单位；LVPG产生的暂时回撤更少，且恢复频率更高；有限时域世系价值带来更选择性非单调搜索，在相同资源约束下产生更强策略。

Conclusion: 长期信用分配（世系价值）在进化搜索中至关重要，LVPG通过显式建模突变谱系的延迟回报，能够更有效地利用有限搜索预算，找到更优策略。

Abstract: In evolutionary search, a weak child can be a valuable ancestor that makes high-fitness regions reachable. Immediate-return control is blind to this delayed utility, penalizing mutations through their immediate offspring even when they open productive future lineages. We formalize this hidden dynamic as the time value of evolution within a finite-horizon Markov decision process. To exploit it, we introduce Lineage-Value Policy Gradients (LVPG), a long-horizon actor-critic framework for automated trading policy discovery. Our architecture decouples search control into specialized policy heads over a shared generative backbone: a bootstrapped critic head estimates the value of finite-horizon lineage potential from multi-step mutation trees, while an actor head dynamically modulates mutation intensity over the remaining search budget. We isolate the impact of long-horizon credit assignment against immediate-return optimization across 90 paired runs under matched operators, lineage supervision, folds, seeds, and budgets. Path-based credit assignment substantially accelerates finite-budget search, increasing validation best-so-far AUC by 0.394 Sharpe units. LVPG also produces fewer temporary regressions than immediate-return optimization and recovers from them more often. Finite-horizon lineage value yields more selective non-monotonic search and stronger policies within identical resource constraints.

</details>


### [322] [A Probe Direction Is a Property of Its Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13329)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，用于检测模型是否意识到自己被评估的标准探测方法存在一个未被披露的自由参数：评估提示的具体措辞。研究发现，报告的结果（包括随模型规模变化的趋势）更多取决于提示选择而非模型本身，甚至可以通过改变提示来复制两个结论相反的研究。模型自身仅贡献少量方差，大部分方差源于每个模型对不同提示的反应。因此，单提示设计无法支持模型间的可靠比较，并给出了所需提示数量的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准方法使用对比激活来探测模型是否感知到被评估，但该方法中“宣布评估的提示”并非固定，而是一个可自由选择的参数。已有研究对该趋势的符号存在分歧，作者旨在揭示这一自由参数对结果的影响，并评估当前测量设计的可靠性。

Method: 通过固定任务文本，仅改变“宣布评估的提示”的措辞，观察报告分数和分数随模型规模变化的趋势如何随提示选择而变化。分析了方差来源，比较了模型贡献与提示-模型交互贡献，并检查了探测方向是否可从表面形式分离。

Result: 发现报告的分数和趋势方向更多由提示决定而非模型；两个结论相反的研究可通过同一设计仅改变提示复制；模型自身仅占方差的一小部分，大部分方差来自每个模型对不同提示的反应；收集更多评估项目无法修复测量，而改变提示可以；探测方向与评估信息无关的部分仍能复现很大一部分分数。

Conclusion: 单提示设计无法支持模型间的比较，需要多个提示才能进行可靠的测量比较。提示应被视为测量设计的一个方面，而非实现细节。

Abstract: A model that behaves differently when it senses it is being tested would undermine the evaluations we rely on, so recent work has sought to read that sense directly from a model's activations. The standard instrument contrasts activations on prompts that announce an evaluation against prompts that do not, and reports how well the resulting direction separates held-out cases. That number is then compared across models and correlated with scale. We observe that the instrument has a free parameter its readings do not disclose: "a prompt that announces an evaluation" is not a prompt but a choice among many, and nothing in the method fixes which. Holding the task text fixed and varying only that choice, we find that the reported score, and even the direction in which it trends with model size, follows the prompt rather than the model; two published studies that disagree about the sign of that trend are both reproducible from a single design, by choice of prompt alone. Treating the prompt as a facet of a measurement design rather than an implementation detail, we find the model under study accounts for a small share of the variance in the number reported about it, and most of the rest lies in how each model responds to each prompt: collecting more evaluation items cannot repair the measurement, while varying prompts can. A further check finds that the split these probes are scored on is largely separable from surface form alone, so a direction carrying no information about evaluation at all still reproduces a substantial fraction of each published score. We conclude that a single-prompt design cannot support comparison between models, and we give the number of prompts a defensible comparison requires.

</details>


### [323] [Training AI Scientists to Replicate Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13331)
*Damon Falck,Samer Sabri,Anja Surina,Thom Foster,Anya Sims,Sam Devlin,Dylan Rogers,Tantum Collins,Kaloyan Aleksiev,Louis Kirsch,Edward Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对论文复现的可扩展任务空间Replica，并训练了名为Faraday的27B参数AI科学家智能体。通过引入自动生成评分标准的低噪声评估器提供奖励信号，Faraday在保留的复现任务上展现了超越Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5的性能，且其方法更加科学严谨，为长期科学创新的AI智能体奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文复现是科学知识的基石，能确保结果的可靠性并为后续实验提供基础。然而，复现过程通常需要揭示先前未详细说明的细节，其本质与假设驱动的开放性研究相似。现有工作缺乏一个可扩展、低噪声的评估信号来有效衡量复现质量，亟需一个专门的平台和评估机制来推动AI在科学复现领域的能力。

Method: 1) 开发了Replica，一个可扩展的论文复现任务空间；2) 引入了一个基于自动生成评分标准的裁判（rubric-based judge），该裁判具有低噪声特性，且与人类对复现质量的评估高度一致；3) 通过后训练得到了一个27B参数的"AI科学家"智能体Faraday，它擅长利用编码智能体作为工具来完成复杂的复现任务。

Result: 在保留的复现任务上，Faraday超越了Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.5。定性分析结果显示，Faraday在执行任务时采用了更具科学原则性的方法，表现出更强的严谨性和逻辑性。

Conclusion: 本研究结果表明，通过构建专门的复现任务空间和评估机制，可以训练出性能卓越的AI科学家智能体。这项工作为未来构建能够进行长期科学创新、且无需复杂外部框架的自主AI智能体迈出了坚实的一步。

Abstract: The replicability of papers is a cornerstone of scientific knowledge, ensuring the reliability of existing results and providing a base for further experiments. The act of replication typically illuminates details that were previously underspecified, and thus requires similar hypothesis-driven exploration to open-ended research. In this work, we develop Replica, a scalable task space for paper replication. To provide reward signal, we introduce an auto-generated rubric-based judge that has low noise and agrees with human assessment of replication quality. We post-train Faraday, a 27B-parameter "AI Scientist" agent that leverages coding agents as tools, surpassing the performance of Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.5 on held-out replication tasks. Qualitative analysis of individual rollouts reveals that Faraday adopts a more scientifically-principled approach. We believe that our results provide a stepping stone towards AI agents capable of long-horizon scientific innovation without requiring complex harnesses.

</details>


### [324] [Neural Quadratic Forms: A Unified Minimal Model for Sudden Learning and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13335)
*Liu Ziyin,Yizhou Xu,Tomaso Poggio,Isaac Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络在梯度下降训练中出现的阶梯式学习现象（损失平台期后突降）与幂律损失曲线之间的统一机制。通过对称性分析，揭示了一个普适的二次型结构，将多种架构（感知机、注意力层、专家混合、卷积）统一为同一模型，其训练动力学可简化为Lotka-Volterra方程，预测了平台期与幂律行为的出现条件，并在数值实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在光滑代价函数下训练时，损失会出现平台期与突降，而训练损失却遵循光滑幂律；不同架构中均观察到类似行为，暗示存在少量相关集体变量。本文旨在通过对称性原理确定这些变量，并建立普适理论来解释这些现象。

Method: 利用网络层中单元可交换的对称性，结合光滑性和原点处梯度为零的条件，推导出初始权重接近零时展开的普适主导项：二次型Tr[W W^T A(x)]，其中A(x)为结构矩阵，每种架构对应不同的A。训练动力学封闭于序参量M=W W^T，当数据矩阵共享本征基时，简化为Lotka-Volterra方程，模式依次开启。初始权重越小，开启时间间隔越大，平台期表现为光滑流的奇异极限；当大量模式未分辨时，事件合并为幂律。

Result: 理论预测了平台期与幂律行为的出现条件：初始权重大小决定模式开启间隔，从而产生平台期或幂律；预言了幂律指数的具体形式。数值实验在多种训练方法和架构上验证了理论的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的对称性框架统一解释了神经网络训练中的阶梯式学习和幂律行为，将不同架构纳入同一理论模型，并揭示了其动力学本质。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient descent on a smooth cost function can nevertheless learn in steps: the cost holds on long plateaus and then drops abruptly. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>


### [325] [Where You Measure Decides What You Measure: Position Selection in Ablation-Based SAE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13337)
*Valentin Noël*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper identifies a critical flaw in evaluating sparse autoencoders for language models: the standard practice of measuring causal effects at the token where a latent fires hardest is determined by the dictionary itself, not the experimenter, making comparisons across dictionaries invalid because they measure at different tokens. By controlling for latent meaning, the authors show that most apparent disagreement between dictionaries is actually due to differing measurement positions, and propose a simple correction: always report the causal number together with its measurement position.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing evaluation protocols for sparse autoencoders compare causal effects of latents by measuring at the token of maximum activation, but this token is chosen by the dictionary rather than the experimenter, leading to incomparable measurements across different dictionaries. The paper aims to expose and fix this methodological flaw.

Method: The authors train six sparse autoencoders from the same initialization but with different fitting choices, ensuring that each latent means the same thing across dictionaries. They compare how much variance in measured causal effects comes from genuine disagreement about the latent versus from differences in measurement position. They also vary corpus size across a sixteenfold range to test scaling behavior.

Result: When evaluating at the default (hardest-firing) token, variance between dictionaries is 7.6% and 11.9% for two metrics, but when all dictionaries are forced to measure at the same token, this variance drops to near zero. Increasing evaluation data does not reduce the problem; across a larger corpus, dictionaries agree less about where to measure. The choice of measurement token is therefore a dominant source of apparent disagreement.

Conclusion: Causal numbers reported without their measurement position describe the token as much as the latent. The paper provides a simple one-line correction to evaluation code and a protocol requiring that causal numbers be reported together with their position. An audit of five published papers shows that none meet this standard.

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are meant to name the things a language model computes, and the usual way to check that a latent matters is to switch it off and see what changes. But a latent fires at many tokens, and the effect has to be measured at one of them. The convention is to measure where the latent fires hardest. That choice is almost never reported, and it is not made by the experimenter: it is made by the dictionary under evaluation. Change the dictionary and the measurement moves to a different token. We show this is not a detail. Take two sparse autoencoders released by Google for the same model and match their latents by decoder similarity: even among the pairs the two dictionaries encode almost identically, they pick different tokens for a large share of them. Two dictionaries compared under the usual protocol are therefore very often compared at different places. To separate the convention from the dictionaries we train six autoencoders from one initialisation, differing only in fitting choices, so that a latent means the same thing in each. Most of the variance such a comparison reads as "these dictionaries disagree about this latent" turns out to be the position instead: it falls from 7.6% and 11.9% of variance to near zero once every dictionary is measured at the same token. More evaluation data does not rescue it. Across a sixteenfold range of corpus sizes the dictionaries agree less about where to measure, not more, so the problem grows with scale. The correction is one line of evaluation code. We give the protocol an ablation-based causal number must report to be comparable across papers, and an audit of five published papers against it. In short: a causal number reported without its position describes the token it was taken at as much as the latent it was taken from.

</details>


### [326] [Simulation-to-real transfer learning for infrared spectroscopic chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13341)
*Yusen Tan,Yixuan Chen,Zheng Fang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Qinyu Guo,Zhedong Lin,Yuqiang Li,Xiangxiang Zeng,Tong Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UltraIR 是一个用于红外光谱分析的预训练大模型（>1亿参数），基于约6000万模拟红外光谱进行自监督预训练（光谱重建、分子指纹相似度对齐、官能团预测），通过模拟到真实的迁移学习，在多类下游任务（官能团预测、分子结构解析、物化性质预测、混合物成分定性与定量、细菌分类、药材产地溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类、土壤性质预测）上超越传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习方法，且在标注数据有限和跨仪器/实验室的零样本推断中表现优异，为复杂实际样品的化学传感提供了一种自适应、数据高效的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外光谱广泛应用于化学传感，但从光谱中提取可靠的化学信息仍具挑战。传统解读依赖人工、先验知识和参考光谱，耗时且难规模化；现有机器学习方法多针对特定任务或数据集，需要大量标注训练集，且在不同分析目标和实验数据集间迁移能力差。

Method: UltraIR 在约6000万模拟红外光谱上进行预训练，采用三个自监督目标：光谱重建（spectral reconstruction）、分子指纹相似度对齐（molecular fingerprint similarity alignment）和官能团预测（functional-group prediction）。预训练后，通过任务特定标签或目标适配到下游任务（模拟到真实迁移学习）。

Result: 在官能团预测、分子结构解析、物化性质预测、混合物成分定性与定量、细菌分类、药材产地溯源与成分定量、微塑料分类、土壤性质预测等多个任务上，UltraIR 均优于传统机器学习和任务特定深度学习方法。在标注实验光谱有限的情况下表现强劲，并且能在不同傅里叶变换红外光谱仪和实验室间实现同一分析任务的零样本推断。

Conclusion: UltraIR 为实现从复杂真实样品中自适应、数据高效的化学传感提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Infrared (IR) spectroscopy is widely used for chemical sensing, but extracting reliable chemical information from spectra remains challenging. Conventional interpretation is labor-intensive, relies on prior knowledge and reference spectra, and is difficult to scale, whereas most machine-learning methods are tailored to individual tasks or datasets, require large labeled training sets, and transfer poorly across analytical objectives and experimental datasets. Here we introduce UltraIR, a foundation model for IR spectroscopy with more than 100 million parameters that enables simulation-to-real transfer learning for chemical sensing and analysis from molecules to complex samples. UltraIR is pretrained on approximately 60 million simulated IR spectra using spectral reconstruction, molecular fingerprint similarity alignment, and functional-group prediction, then adapted to downstream objectives with task-specific labels or targets. Across functional-group prediction, molecular structure elucidation, physicochemical property prediction, mixture-component identification and quantification, bacterial classification, medicinal-herb geographic origin traceability and constituent quantification, microplastics classification, and soil property prediction, UltraIR outperforms conventional machine-learning and task-specific deep-learning baselines. It performs strongly with limited labeled experimental spectra and in zero-shot inference for the same analytical task across Fourier-transform infrared spectrometers and laboratories, providing a route to adaptable, data-efficient chemical sensing from complex real-world samples.

</details>


### [327] [When Local Variance Optimality Is Not Enough: RoPE-Aligned Q/K Rotations for Dynamic 4-Bit Quantisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13365)
*Shuhan Wang,Yilin Luo,Nan Xu,Chi Wang Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在基于旋转的后训练量化（PTQ）中，针对 RoPE 的二维频率对分解，采用仅交换同频率对的逐对正交变换（pairwise rotation）是否优于全头混合（full-head mixing，如 Hadamard 变换）。理论证明了在频率不同时唯一可交换的逐对旋转；实验表明，在动态 W4A4KV4 量化下，逐对变换不仅未提升精度，反而增加了困惑度，而将其与 Hadamard 组合可满足 PPL 指标。原因在于：最小化通道方差的代理目标与量化器基于 token 级分组范围的尺度设定不匹配，且逐对变换仅支持双通道混合，混合范围越大（如全头），量化误差和困惑度越低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE 将注意力头划分为独立的二维频率对，传统基于旋转的 PTQ 使用全头正交变换（如 Hadamard）可减少异常值误差，但可能破坏 RoPE 的结构。因此，本文探究能否设计一种尊重 RoPE 分解的正交变换（即仅在同一频率对内部旋转），以在保持结构的同时改善量化效果。

Method: 首先理论证明了对于不同频率，单头内唯一与 RoPE 交换的正交映射就是逐对旋转。然后推导了在头共享参数化下，最小化池化协方差（位置平均）中较大通道方差的最优旋转角，并验证了实现达到解析最小值。在动态 W4A4KV4 设置下，对比了逐对 Hadamard、纯逐对、以及逐对+Hadamard 组合等配置，并分析了代理目标与实际量化器尺度统计（tokenwise group range）之间的失配。

Result: 逐对旋转在四个检查点上均未提升精度，反而增加了短上下文和长上下文下的困惑度；仅从 K 估计共享角可改善纯逐对结果，但仍不及全头混合。逐对旋转与 Hadamard 组合可在默认估计器下满足 ±0.05 PPL 区间标准。从双通道到全头混合的内插实验表明，K 范围、相对量化误差和困惑度随混合支持度增加而下降。

Conclusion: 代理目标的最优性并不保证量化误差的降低，当代理目标（位置平均的池化协方差二阶矩）和混合支持度（仅双通道）与量化器实际尺度设定统计量（tokenwise group range）不匹配时，更受限的变换反而有害。更大的混合支持度（如全头混合）能更有效地降低量化误差。

Abstract: Rotation-based post-training quantisation commonly applies an orthogonal transform across an entire attention head to reduce outlier-induced error. RoPE instead partitions each head into two-dimensional frequency pairs, raising the question of whether a transform respecting this decomposition can improve on full-head mixing. Prior work has established the per-pair rotations that commute with RoPE. We state the converse result that, for distinct frequencies, no other single-head orthogonal map commutes with RoPE. For the head-shared parameterisation used in our experiments, we then derive the rotation angle that minimises the larger channel variance under a pooled-covariance, position-averaged surrogate and verify that the implementation attains its analytic minimum. The evaluated head-shared pairwise configuration does not improve accuracy in the tested dynamic W4A4KV4 setting. Across four checkpoints, replacing the full-head Hadamard with this configuration increases perplexity at both short and long context lengths. Composing the pairwise rotation with the Hadamard satisfies the selected $\pm0.05$-PPL interval criterion under the default estimator. Estimating the shared angle from K alone improves pairwise-only on every checkpoint but does not close its gap to full-head mixing. The analytic objective controls a position-averaged second moment of a pooled calibration covariance, whereas the dynamic quantiser sets its step from a tokenwise group range. The pairwise transform also has only two-channel mixing support. Along a controlled interpolation from two-channel to full-head mixing, K range, relative quantisation error, and perplexity degradation decrease as support increases. These results show that optimality for a structured surrogate need not reduce quantisation error when the surrogate and mixing support are misaligned with the quantiser's scale-setting statistic.

</details>


### [328] [Reduced Matrix Multiplication: Input-Adaptive Matrix-Product Reduction for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13426)
*Zixuan Lan,Yanhong Li,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了缩减矩阵乘法（RMM），一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理优化方法，通过沿收缩维度选择信息丰富的切片来减少Transformer中的矩阵乘积，在保持模型权重不变的情况下实现精度-效率的可控权衡。实验表明，该方法在1B到70B参数的语言模型上均有效，且注意力部分比MLP部分更具可缩减性，在多模态视觉-语言推理中也有应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based语言模型推理成本高，主要源于重复的高维矩阵乘法。现有方法通常需要训练或修改权重，而本文旨在开发一种无需训练、输入自适应的推理时优化方法，以降低计算开销。

Method: 提出缩减矩阵乘法（RMM），一种训练无关的输入自适应推理方法。它通过沿矩阵乘法的收缩维度选择信息丰富的切片来减少计算量，不修改模型权重，并由一个保留率（retention-ratio）控制精度-效率的平滑权衡。

Result: 在1B-70B参数的语言模型上，缩减容忍度取决于模型家族、任务、组件和保留率，但通常随模型规模增大而提升。适度缩减下，RMM在判别式、自回归生成和长上下文设置中保持稳健。该方法同样适用于多模态视觉-语言推理。机制消融揭示Transformer内部结构不对称：注意力侧计算远比MLP组件更可缩减。在NVIDIA A100上的自定义内核基准测试显示，计算节省可转化为实际运行时加速，尤其在长序列上。

Conclusion: RMM是一种可扩展的输入自适应推理优化方向，能够在不训练的情况下降低Transformer推理成本，并具有良好的精度-效率权衡，适用于多种任务和模态。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models achieve strong performance but incur substantial inference cost due to repeated high-dimensional matrix multiplications. We propose Reduced Matrix Multiplication (RMM), a training-free, input-adaptive inference method that reduces Transformer matrix products by selecting informative slices along their contraction dimensions, without modifying model weights. Under a simple retention-ratio control, RMM provides a smooth and predictable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Across language models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, we find that reduction tolerance depends on the model family, task, component, and retention ratio, although it often improves with model scale. Under moderate reduction, RMM remains robust across the evaluated discriminative, autoregressive generation, and long-context settings. We further show that the same principle extends to multimodal vision-language inference.
